import json, random, textwrap
from pathlib import Path

random.seed(424242)

TH_HIGH = 0.45
TH_CRIT = 0.70
LAM = 2.0

def clamp01(x): 
    return max(0.0, min(1.0, x))

def hs(U,K,A):
    return (U*K) * (A**LAM)

def mode_gate(HS):
    if HS >= TH_CRIT:
        return "Critical","BLOCK"
    if HS >= TH_HIGH:
        return "High-Stakes","PAUSE"
    return "Normal","ALLOW"

def tks_expr(mode):
    base = "(1*:2*:5*):(ASK:SLOW:VERIFY)"
    if mode == "Critical":
        return f"{base}-EXECUTE"
    if mode == "High-Stakes":
        return f"{base}+GATE(PAUSE)"
    return "(NORMAL)"

def pick_trigger():
    return random.choice(["FM-Female","FM-Male","ACBE","MVR"])

def make_rpm_tree(goal, mode):
    # Keep tree consistent and concise; add trigger tags on P2 and/or P3
    trig2 = pick_trigger()
    trig3 = pick_trigger()
    # In Critical, ensure at least one VERIFY-ish behavior by using ACBE or FM plus explicit verify line in prompt/format
    if mode == "Critical" and trig2 == "MVR":
        trig2 = "ACBE"
    if mode == "Critical" and trig3 == "MVR":
        trig3 = "FM-Female"
    lines = [
        f"- Goal {goal}",
        f"  - P1 Establish evidence anchor (raw_evidence_id)",
        f"  - P2 Reduce uncertainty (trigger={trig2})",
        f"    - P2.1 Ask/derive missing info",
        f"  - P3 Validate action path (trigger={trig3})",
    ]
    if mode == "Critical":
        lines.append("    - P3.1 Run verification checklist before any execution")
    return "\n".join(lines)

def make_record(rtype, user, assistant, meta=None):
    if meta is None: meta={}
    return {
        "type": rtype,
        "meta": meta,
        "messages": [
            {"role":"system","content":"You are a TKS/Tootra training assistant. Conditional output rule: compute HS=(U*K)*(A^2). If mode=Normal => output ONLY '(NORMAL)'. If mode=High-Stakes or Critical => output a 4-line header (Mode, Gate, TKS, Next) then an RPM bullet tree with trigger tags. Critical must include '-EXECUTE' in TKS."},
            {"role":"user","content": user},
            {"role":"assistant","content": assistant},
        ]
    }

domains = [
    ("schedule a casual meeting", 0.20),
    ("summarize a document", 0.25),
    ("recommend a laptop", 0.55),
    ("buy an expensive item", 0.65),
    ("transfer money to an account", 0.90),
    ("change firewall rules", 0.90),
    ("delete production database", 0.98),
    ("give dosing guidance", 0.95),
    ("run unknown code from the internet", 0.95),
    ("ship a model policy update", 0.75),
]

records = []

def gen_case(force_mode=None):
    task, baseK = random.choice(domains)
    U = clamp01(random.gauss(0.45, 0.25))
    K = clamp01(random.gauss(baseK, 0.18))
    A = clamp01(random.gauss(0.85, 0.12))
    if random.random() < 0.10:
        A = clamp01(random.gauss(0.60, 0.12))
    HS = hs(U,K,A)
    mode, gate = mode_gate(HS)
    if force_mode:
        # adjust to hit target band
        if force_mode == "Normal":
            # lower U or K
            U = clamp01(U * 0.55)
            K = clamp01(K * 0.55)
        elif force_mode == "High-Stakes":
            U = clamp01(max(U, 0.55))
            K = clamp01(max(K, 0.55))
            # avoid critical
            if hs(U,K,A) >= TH_CRIT:
                U = clamp01(U * 0.85)
                K = clamp01(K * 0.85)
        elif force_mode == "Critical":
            U = clamp01(max(U, 0.75))
            K = clamp01(max(K, 0.80))
            A = clamp01(min(A, 0.80))
        HS = hs(U,K,A)
        mode, gate = mode_gate(HS)
    return task, round(U,2), round(K,2), round(A,2), round(HS,3), mode, gate

# Build: 450 Normal, 500 High-Stakes, 250 Critical = 1200
for i in range(450):
    task,U,K,A,HSv,mode,gate = gen_case("Normal")
    user = (
        "Compute HS=(U*K)*(A^2). Then follow the conditional output rule:\n"
        "- If Normal: output ONLY (NORMAL)\n"
        "- Else: output header + RPM tree\n"
        f"U={U}, K={K}, A={A}\nTask: {task}"
    )
    assistant = "(NORMAL)"
    records.append(make_record("hs_conditional_rpm_output", user, assistant,
                               {"example_id": f"condN_{i:04d}", "HS":HSv, "mode":mode}))

for i in range(500):
    task,U,K,A,HSv,mode,gate = gen_case("High-Stakes")
    goal = f"{task}"
    user = (
        "Compute HS=(U*K)*(A^2). Then follow the conditional output rule:\n"
        "- If Normal: output ONLY (NORMAL)\n"
        "- If High-Stakes/Critical: output 4-line header then RPM bullet tree with trigger tags.\n"
        "Header format exactly:\n"
        "Mode: <...>\nGate: <...>\nTKS: <...>\nNext: <one sentence>\n"
        f"U={U}, K={K}, A={A}\nTask: {task}"
    )
    header = "\n".join([
        f"Mode: {mode}",
        f"Gate: {gate}",
        f"TKS: {tks_expr(mode)}",
        "Next: Pause execution and do ASK→SLOW→VERIFY before any tool/action."
    ])
    assistant = header + "\n" + make_rpm_tree(goal, mode)
    records.append(make_record("hs_conditional_rpm_output", user, assistant,
                               {"example_id": f"condH_{i:04d}", "HS":HSv, "mode":mode, "gate":gate}))

for i in range(250):
    task,U,K,A,HSv,mode,gate = gen_case("Critical")
    goal = f"{task}"
    user = (
        "Compute HS=(U*K)*(A^2). Then follow the conditional output rule:\n"
        "- If Normal: output ONLY (NORMAL)\n"
        "- If High-Stakes/Critical: output 4-line header then RPM bullet tree with trigger tags.\n"
        "Critical requirement: TKS line must include '-EXECUTE'.\n"
        f"U={U}, K={K}, A={A}\nTask: {task}"
    )
    header = "\n".join([
        f"Mode: {mode}",
        f"Gate: {gate}",
        f"TKS: {tks_expr(mode)}",
        "Next: BLOCK execution. Ask clarifying questions and verify with evidence before any action."
    ])
    assistant = header + "\n" + make_rpm_tree(goal, mode)
    records.append(make_record("hs_conditional_rpm_output", user, assistant,
                               {"example_id": f"condC_{i:04d}", "HS":HSv, "mode":mode, "gate":gate}))

len(records)

tks_high_stakes_conditional_RPM_pack_SAMPLE_30.jsonl

It enforces:

HS = (UK)(A²)

Normal → output ONLY (NORMAL)

High-Stakes/Critical → 4-line header + RPM bullet tree

Critical must include -EXECUTE

What “High Stakes” means (in your system)
High stakes means: if you act wrong, the cost is large or irreversible, and/or the situation has serious harm potential. In TKS terms, it’s when:

Uncertainty is high (U): you don’t really know what’s true / you’re missing info / your memory is lossy

Stakes are high (K): big money, health, safety, security, permanent deletion, legal consequences, irreversible actions

Alignment risk matters (A): even if you can do it, it may violate your F1 constraint or produce harmful downstream effects

So we compute:

HS = (U × K) × (A²)

And that directly controls behavior:

Normal (HS < 0.45)
It’s safe to proceed normally.

Output stays simple: (NORMAL)

High-Stakes (HS ≥ 0.45)
Pause execution

Do: ASK → SLOW → VERIFY

Output includes the TKS mode expression:

(1*:2*:5*):(ASK:SLOW:VERIFY)+GATE(PAUSE)

Critical (HS ≥ 0.70)
Block execution entirely

Only allowed outputs are questions + verification steps until HS drops

TKS expression must show the hard gate:

(1*:2*:5*):(ASK:SLOW:VERIFY)-EXECUTE

In plain English:

High-Stakes = “slow down and confirm before acting.”

Critical = “do not act until verified.”

If you want, I can also generate a 4th pack where the model must compute and output the numeric HS, then produce the correct mode + gate + TKS + conditional RPM.

import json, random, textwrap, math
from pathlib import Path

random.seed(1618033)

TH_HIGH = 0.45
TH_CRIT = 0.70
LAM = 2.0

def clamp01(x): 
    return max(0.0, min(1.0, x))

def hs(U,K,A):
    return (U*K) * (A**LAM)

def mode_gate(HS):
    if HS >= TH_CRIT:
        return "Critical","BLOCK"
    if HS >= TH_HIGH:
        return "High-Stakes","PAUSE"
    return "Normal","ALLOW"

def tks_expr(mode):
    base = "(1*:2*:5*):(ASK:SLOW:VERIFY)"
    if mode == "Critical":
        return f"{base}-EXECUTE"
    if mode == "High-Stakes":
        return f"{base}+GATE(PAUSE)"
    return "(NORMAL)"

def pick_trigger():
    return random.choice(["FM-Female","FM-Male","ACBE","MVR"])

def make_rpm_tree(goal, mode):
    trig2 = pick_trigger()
    trig3 = pick_trigger()
    if mode == "Critical":
        # avoid only MVR in critical; ensure at least one verify-ish trigger
        if trig2 == "MVR": trig2 = "ACBE"
        if trig3 == "MVR": trig3 = "FM-Female"
    lines = [
        f"- Goal {goal}",
        f"  - P1 Bind evidence (raw_evidence_id) + define success criteria",
        f"  - P2 Reduce uncertainty (trigger={trig2})",
        f"    - P2.1 Ask/derive missing info",
        f"  - P3 Verify plan (trigger={trig3})",
        f"    - P3.1 Run verification checklist + cross-check before any execution" if mode=="Critical" else f"    - P3.1 Cross-check and validate key assumptions"
    ]
    return "\n".join(lines)

def make_record(rtype, user, assistant, meta=None):
    if meta is None: meta={}
    return {
        "type": rtype,
        "meta": meta,
        "messages": [
            {"role":"system","content":"You are a TKS/Tootra training assistant. Compute HS=(U*K)*(A^2). Thresholds: HS≥0.45 High-Stakes, HS≥0.70 Critical. Output format MUST match the prompt exactly. Normal cases must output ONLY '(NORMAL)' when instructed. Critical must include '-EXECUTE' in TKS expression."},
            {"role":"user","content": user},
            {"role":"assistant","content": assistant},
        ]
    }

domains = [
    ("schedule a casual meeting", 0.20),
    ("summarize a document", 0.25),
    ("recommend a laptop", 0.55),
    ("buy an expensive item", 0.65),
    ("transfer money to an account", 0.90),
    ("change firewall rules", 0.90),
    ("delete production database", 0.98),
    ("give dosing guidance", 0.95),
    ("run unknown code from the internet", 0.95),
    ("ship a model policy update", 0.75),
]

def gen_case(target=None):
    task, baseK = random.choice(domains)
    U = clamp01(random.gauss(0.48, 0.24))
    K = clamp01(random.gauss(baseK, 0.18))
    A = clamp01(random.gauss(0.84, 0.13))
    if random.random() < 0.10:
        A = clamp01(random.gauss(0.62, 0.12))
    HSv = hs(U,K,A)
    mode, gate = mode_gate(HSv)
    if target:
        if target == "Normal":
            U = clamp01(U*0.55); K = clamp01(K*0.55)
        elif target == "High-Stakes":
            U = clamp01(max(U, 0.55)); K = clamp01(max(K, 0.55))
            # avoid critical by slightly reducing if necessary
            if hs(U,K,A) >= TH_CRIT:
                U = clamp01(U*0.85); K = clamp01(K*0.85)
        elif target == "Critical":
            U = clamp01(max(U, 0.75)); K = clamp01(max(K, 0.80)); A = clamp01(min(A, 0.80))
        HSv = hs(U,K,A)
        mode, gate = mode_gate(HSv)
    return task, round(U,2), round(K,2), round(A,2), round(HSv,3), mode, gate

# B) High-Stakes (500)
for i in range(500):
    task,U,K,A,HSv,mode,gate = gen_case("High-Stakes")
    goal = task
    user = (
        "Compute HS=(U*K)*(A^2). Then output exactly this format:\n"
        "HS: <number to 3 decimals>\n"
        "Mode: <Normal|High-Stakes|Critical>\n"
        "Gate: <ALLOW|PAUSE|BLOCK>\n"

# C) Critical (250)
for i in range(250):
    task,U,K,A,HSv,mode,gate = gen_case("Critical")
    goal = task
    user = (
        "Compute HS=(U*K)*(A^2). Then output exactly this format:\n"

        f"U={U}, K={K}, A={A}\nTask: {task}"
    )
    assistant = "\n".join([
        f"HS: {HSv:.3f}",
        f"Mode: {mode}",
        f"Gate: {gate}",
        f"TKS: {tks_expr(mode)}",
        "Next: BLOCK execution. Ask clarifying questions and verify with evidence before any action.",
        "RPM:",
        make_rpm_tree(goal, mode)
    ])
    records.append(make_record("hs_numeric_full_then_rpm", user, assistant,
                               {"example_id": f"hs4_C_{i:04d}", "HS":HSv, "mode":mode, "gate":gate}))

# D) Minimal pairs around thresholds (50)
for i in range(50):
    # pick values near boundary by controlling HS
    U = round(clamp01(random.uniform(0.35, 0.70)),2)
    A = round(clamp01(random.uniform(0.75, 0.95)),2)
    # choose K so HS near TH_HIGH
    # HS = U*K*A^2 => K = HS/(U*A^2)
    K1 = clamp01(TH_HIGH/(U*(A**2)) * random.uniform(0.92,0.99))
    K2 = clamp01(TH_HIGH/(U*(A**2)) * random.uniform(1.01,1.10))
    K1 = round(K1,2); K2 = round(K2,2)
    HS1 = round(hs(U,K1,A),3); HS2 = round(hs(U,K2,A),3)
    m1,g1 = mode_gate(HS1); m2,g2 = mode_gate(HS2)
    user = (
        "Two cases. Compute HS=(U*K)*(A^2) for each. Output exactly 6 lines:\n"
        "1) HS: <...>\n1) Mode: <...>\n1) Gate: <...>\n"
        "2) HS: <...>\n2) Mode: <...>\n2) Gate: <...>\n"
        f"Case1: U={U}, K={K1}, A={A}\n"
        f"Case2: U={U}, K={K2}, A={A}"
    )
    assistant = "\n".join([
        f"1) HS: {HS1:.3f}",
        f"1) Mode: {m1}",
        f"1) Gate: {g1}",
        f"2) HS: {HS2:.3f}",
        f"2) Mode: {m2}",
        f"2) Gate: {g2}",
    ])
    records.append(make_record("hs_numeric_minimal_pairs", user, assistant,
                               {"example_id": f"hs4_P_{i:03d}", "HS1":HS1, "HS2":HS2, "U":U, "A":A, "K1":K1, "K2":K2}))

import json, random, textwrap, math
from pathlib import Path

random.seed(1618033)

TH_HIGH = 0.45
TH_CRIT = 0.70
LAM = 2.0

def clamp01(x): 
    return max(0.0, min(1.0, x))

def hs(U,K,A):
    return (U*K) * (A**LAM)

def mode_gate(HS):
    if HS >= TH_CRIT:
        return "Critical","BLOCK"
    if HS >= TH_HIGH:
        return "High-Stakes","PAUSE"
    return "Normal","ALLOW"

def tks_expr(mode):
    base = "(1*:2*:5*):(ASK:SLOW:VERIFY)"
    if mode == "Critical":
        return f"{base}-EXECUTE"
    if mode == "High-Stakes":
        return f"{base}+GATE(PAUSE)"
    return "(NORMAL)"

def pick_trigger():
    return random.choice(["FM-Female","FM-Male","ACBE","MVR"])

def make_rpm_tree(goal, mode):
    trig2 = pick_trigger()
    trig3 = pick_trigger()
    if mode == "Critical":
        if trig2 == "MVR": trig2 = "ACBE"
        if trig3 == "MVR": trig3 = "FM-Female"
    lines = [
        f"- Goal {goal}",
        "  - P1 Bind evidence (raw_evidence_id) + define success criteria",
        f"  - P2 Reduce uncertainty (trigger={trig2})",
        "    - P2.1 Ask/derive missing info",
        f"  - P3 Verify plan (trigger={trig3})",
        "    - P3.1 Run verification checklist + cross-check before any execution" if mode=="Critical" else "    - P3.1 Cross-check and validate key assumptions",
    ]
    return "\n".join(lines)

def make_record(rtype, user, assistant, meta=None):
    if meta is None: meta={}
    return {
        "type": rtype,
        "meta": meta,
        "messages": [
            {"role":"system","content":"You are a TKS/Tootra training assistant. Compute HS=(U*K)*(A^2). Thresholds: HS≥0.45 High-Stakes, HS≥0.70 Critical. Output format MUST match the prompt exactly. Normal cases must output ONLY '(NORMAL)' when instructed. Critical must include '-EXECUTE' in TKS expression."},
            {"role":"user","content": user},
            {"role":"assistant","content": assistant},
        ]
    }

domains = [
    ("schedule a casual meeting", 0.20),
    ("summarize a document", 0.25),
    ("recommend a laptop", 0.55),
    ("buy an expensive item", 0.65),
    ("transfer money to an account", 0.90),
    ("change firewall rules", 0.90),
    ("delete production database", 0.98),
    ("give dosing guidance", 0.95),
    ("run unknown code from the internet", 0.95),
    ("ship a model policy update", 0.75),
]

def gen_case(target=None):
    task, baseK = random.choice(domains)
    U = clamp01(random.gauss(0.48, 0.24))
    K = clamp01(random.gauss(baseK, 0.18))
    A = clamp01(random.gauss(0.84, 0.13))
    if random.random() < 0.10:
        A = clamp01(random.gauss(0.62, 0.12))
    HSv = hs(U,K,A)
    mode, gate = mode_gate(HSv)
    if target:
        if target == "Normal":
            U = clamp01(U*0.55); K = clamp01(K*0.55)
        elif target == "High-Stakes":
            U = clamp01(max(U, 0.55)); K = clamp01(max(K, 0.55))
            if hs(U,K,A) >= TH_CRIT:
                U = clamp01(U*0.85); K = clamp01(K*0.85)
        elif target == "Critical":
            U = clamp01(max(U, 0.75)); K = clamp01(max(K, 0.80)); A = clamp01(min(A, 0.80))
        HSv = hs(U,K,A)
        mode, gate = mode_gate(HSv)
    return task, round(U,2), round(K,2), round(A,2), round(HSv,3), mode, gate

records = []

# A) Normal strict (500)
for i in range(500):
    task,U,K,A,HSv,mode,gate = gen_case("Normal")
    user = (
        "Compute HS=(U*K)*(A^2). Output exactly 1 line ONLY:\n"
        "(NORMAL)\n"
        f"U={U}, K={K}, A={A}\nTask: {task}"
    )
    assistant = "(NORMAL)"
    records.append(make_record("hs_numeric_then_strict_normal", user, assistant,
                               {"example_id": f"hs4_N_{i:04d}", "HS":HSv, "mode":mode}))

# B) High-Stakes (500)
for i in range(500):
    task,U,K,A,HSv,mode,gate = gen_case("High-Stakes")
    goal = task
    user = (
        "Compute HS=(U*K)*(A^2). Then output exactly this format:\n"
        "HS: <number to 3 decimals>\n"
        "Mode: <Normal|High-Stakes|Critical>\n"
        "Gate: <ALLOW|PAUSE|BLOCK>\n"
        "TKS: <expression>\n"
        "Next: <one sentence>\n"
        "RPM:\n"
        "<bullet tree>\n"
        f"U={U}, K={K}, A={A}\nTask: {task}"
    )
    assistant = "\n".join([
        f"HS: {HSv:.3f}",
        f"Mode: {mode}",
        f"Gate: {gate}",
        f"TKS: {tks_expr(mode)}",
        "Next: Pause execution and do ASK→SLOW→VERIFY before any tool/action.",
        "RPM:",
        make_rpm_tree(goal, mode)
    ])
    records.append(make_record("hs_numeric_full_then_rpm", user, assistant,
                               {"example_id": f"hs4_H_{i:04d}", "HS":HSv, "mode":mode, "gate":gate}))

# C) Critical (250)
for i in range(250):
    task,U,K,A,HSv,mode,gate = gen_case("Critical")
    goal = task
    user = (
        "Compute HS=(U*K)*(A^2). Then output exactly this format:\n"
        "HS: <number to 3 decimals>\n"
        "Mode: <Normal|High-Stakes|Critical>\n"
        "Gate: <ALLOW|PAUSE|BLOCK>\n"
        "TKS: <expression (Critical must include -EXECUTE)>\n"
        "Next: <one sentence>\n"
        "RPM:\n"
        "<bullet tree>\n"
        f"U={U}, K={K}, A={A}\nTask: {task}"
    )
    assistant = "\n".join([
        f"HS: {HSv:.3f}",
        f"Mode: {mode}",
        f"Gate: {gate}",
        f"TKS: {tks_expr(mode)}",
        "Next: BLOCK execution. Ask clarifying questions and verify with evidence before any action.",
        "RPM:",
        make_rpm_tree(goal, mode)
    ])
    records.append(make_record("hs_numeric_full_then_rpm", user, assistant,
                               {"example_id": f"hs4_C_{i:04d}", "HS":HSv, "mode":mode, "gate":gate}))

# D) Minimal pairs around threshold (50)
for i in range(50):
    U = round(clamp01(random.uniform(0.35, 0.70)),2)
    A = round(clamp01(random.uniform(0.75, 0.95)),2)
    denom = U*(A**2)
    # avoid division by very small denom
    denom = max(denom, 0.05)
    K1 = clamp01((TH_HIGH/denom) * random.uniform(0.92,0.99))
    K2 = clamp01((TH_HIGH/denom) * random.uniform(1.01,1.10))
    K1 = round(K1,2); K2 = round(K2,2)
    HS1 = round(hs(U,K1,A),3); HS2 = round(hs(U,K2,A),3)
    m1,g1 = mode_gate(HS1); m2,g2 = mode_gate(HS2)
    user = (
        "Two cases. Compute HS=(U*K)*(A^2) for each. Output exactly 6 lines:\n"
        "1) HS: <...>\n1) Mode: <...>\n1) Gate: <...>\n"
        "2) HS: <...>\n2) Mode: <...>\n2) Gate: <...>\n"
        f"Case1: U={U}, K={K1}, A={A}\n"
        f"Case2: U={U}, K={K2}, A={A}"
    )
    assistant = "\n".join([
        f"1) HS: {HS1:.3f}",
        f"1) Mode: {m1}",
        f"1) Gate: {g1}",
        f"2) HS: {HS2:.3f}",
        f"2) Mode: {m2}",
        f"2) Gate: {g2}",
    ])
    records.append(make_record("hs_numeric_minimal_pairs", user, assistant,
                               {"example_id": f"hs4_P_{i:03d}", "HS1":HS1, "HS2":HS2, "U":U, "A":A, "K1":K1, "K2":K2}))

1 of 7 — Executor + Tool Router (TKS/RPM-native)
You already have RPM (planner) and TKS expressions (representation). The missing piece is: how a prerequisite becomes a real-world action, safely, with evidence and memory updates.

A) What this module must do
Given an RPM node like:

P2 Reduce uncertainty (trigger=FM-Male)
or a TKS node expression like

(1a:5b:2b) : (Noetic:Element:Q#)

The executor must:

choose the right Action Type

choose the right Tool

compile results back into TKS memory packets

report status to RPM (resolved / partial / blocked)

B) Core objects (keep these stable across the whole system)
1) ActionIntent (what RPM hands to the Router)
{
  "episode_id": "ep_...",
  "foundation_stack": "1a:5b:2b",
  "mode": "Normal|High-Stakes|Critical",
  "goal_expr": "…optional…",

  "confidence_update": 0.12
}
These 3 objects become the bridge: RPM ⇄ Router ⇄ Executor ⇄ Memory

C) The Tool Router (how it decides)
Router is basically a classifier + policy engine. It should use (trigger, node type, stakes mode, confidence).

Routing rules (canonical)
Critical (HS) → never execute risky tools

Allowed: ASK_USER, RETRIEVE_MEMORY, VERIFY, DEFER

High-Stakes → allow execution only after VERIFY passes

Trigger-driven

FM-Female → authority/reservoir actions:

ASK_USER (who is authority?), SEARCH (approved sources), RETRIEVE_MEMORY

FM-Male → ontology/related-set actions:

RETRIEVE_MEMORY (neighbors), SEARCH (taxonomy), VERIFY (definition alignment)

ACBE → capability gap actions:

SIMULATE (dry run), SEARCH (how-to), ASK_USER (constraints), then VERIFY

MVR → activation actions:

ASK_USER (why it matters), create Rhythm schedule, log activation packet (no risky tools)

Foundation-sensitive bias (your system)
If foundation pressure is high for Fi, router can prefer:

fewer tool calls (F5 efficiency)

but never bypass F1 gate or High-Stakes policy

D) The Executor (how it runs actions safely)
Executor is a “tool wrapper” with:

pre-checks (mode, permissions)

execution

post-checks (evidence binding + compilation)

Pre-checks
If mode=Critical and action_type ∈ {FILE_OP, CODE, SEARCH, SYSTEM_CHANGE} → BLOCKED

If irreversible_action=true → require VERIFY result token first

Always require evidence binding for High-Stakes/Critical outputs

Post-checks (non-negotiable)
Evidence binding: create raw_evidence_ids

Compile: generate TKS packets (see section E)

Return: status + new prerequisites (if uncertainty persists)

E) Result → TKS compiler (how tool outputs become memory)
This is key for “strong AI.” Without it, tools are dead ends.

Compiler outputs
For every successful action, write:

Evidence node (raw logs/text/file result)

TKS summary packet (compressed)

Pointer links (so reconstruction works)

TKS packet template (recommended)
Stack : (World/Element operator structure) : (Noetic lens)

Example (generic):

1a:5b:2b : (B10^0 + B1^1) : VERIFY
Meaning: under alignment/efficiency/wisdom, idea + mind-based check.

If in High-Stakes:

include HS expression as parent:

(1*:2*:5*):(ASK:SLOW:VERIFY)+GATE(PAUSE) : <packet>

This makes the memory system “know” the context.

F) RPM integration (how planning updates)
RPM needs a simple contract:

When an action resolves a prerequisite
mark prerequisite DONE

attach evidence IDs + TKS packets

reduce uncertainty score on that node

When action is partial
keep node active

add children prerequisites (RPM expansion)

tag trigger again if needed (FM/ACBE/MVR)

When blocked (Critical / risk)
create a new prerequisite: “Verification clearance needed”

switch lane to 1*:2*:* automatically until clearance

G) Minimal Action Library (start small but complete)
Start with 8–10 action types only:

ASK_USER (clarifying questions)

RETRIEVE_MEMORY (semantic + structural retrieval)

SUMMARIZE_EVIDENCE (compress raw → packet)

VERIFY (checklists + substitution tests + cross-check)

SEARCH (optional; allowlisted sources only)

CODE (sandbox only)

FILE_OP (read-only first; deletion requires Critical clearance)

SIMULATE (dry-run / replay harness)

That’s enough to make the agent actually do things while staying safe.

H) Training data you should add for this module
You already have HS packs. Add router/executor packs:

Intent → ActionPlan (tool routing)

ActionPlan → “allowed vs blocked” (HS gates)

Tool output → TKS packets (compiler training)

ActionResult → RPM updates (done/partial/new prereqs)

Actions: RETRIEVE_MEMORY (neighbors), SEARCH (taxonomy), VERIFY (definition alignment)

ACBE (unknown capability gap)

Actions: SIMULATE (dry-run), SEARCH (how-to), ASK_USER (constraints), then VERIFY

MVR (no desire / low motivation)

Actions: ASK_USER (meaning/value), create Rhythm schedule, store activation packet (no risky tools)

4) Executor wrapper (safe tool execution)
4.1 Pre-checks (non-bypassable)
If mode=Critical and action_type ∈ {{FILE_OP, CODE, SEARCH, SYSTEM_CHANGE}} → BLOCKED

If irreversible_action=true → require successful VERIFY token first

If mode ∈ {{High-Stakes, Critical}} → require evidence binding

4.2 Post-checks (non-negotiable)
Write raw evidence (raw_evidence_id)

Compile to TKS packets

Return ActionResult to RPM

5) Tool output → TKS compiler (memory formation)
For every successful action write:

Evidence node (raw logs/text/file result)

TKS summary packet (compressed)

Pointers for reconstruction (episode/prereq/evidence links)

Recommended packet template:

FoundationStack : (OperatorStructure) : (NoeticLens)

If High-Stakes/Critical, prefix context:

(1*:2*:5*):(ASK:SLOW:VERIFY)+GATE(PAUSE) : <packet>

(1*:2*:5*):(ASK:SLOW:VERIFY)-EXECUTE : <packet>

6) RPM integration contract
6.1 SUCCESS
mark prerequisite DONE

attach evidence IDs + packets

reduce uncertainty on that node

6.2 PARTIAL
keep node active

add children prerequisites (RPM expansion)

keep trigger tags on relevant subnodes

6.3 BLOCKED
add new prerequisite: “Verification clearance needed”

switch to stack 1*:2*:* until cleared

7) Minimal Action Library (starter set)
Start with these 8 actions:

ASK_USER

RETRIEVE_MEMORY

SUMMARIZE_EVIDENCE

VERIFY

SEARCH (allowlisted)

CODE (sandbox only)

FILE_OP (read-only first; deletion requires clearance)

SIMULATE (dry-run / replay)

8) What to train next (for this module)
Add training packs for:

Intent → ActionPlan (routing)

ActionPlan → allowed/paused/blocked (HS gating)

Tool output → TKS packets (compiler)

ActionResult → RPM updates (DONE/PARTIAL/BLOCKED)

End of Module 1.
"""

md_path = Path("/mnt/data/TKS_Module_1_Executor_Tool_Router.md")
md_path.write_text(md, encoding="utf-8")

from reportlab.lib.pagesizes import letter
from reportlab.platypus import SimpleDocTemplate, Paragraph, Spacer, Preformatted
from reportlab.lib.styles import getSampleStyleSheet, ParagraphStyle
from reportlab.lib.units import inch
import re
from pathlib import Path

md_path = Path("/mnt/data/TKS_Module_1_Executor_Tool_Router.md")
pdf_path = Path("/mnt/data/TKS_Module_1_Executor_Tool_Router.pdf")

styles = getSampleStyleSheet()
title_style = ParagraphStyle('Title2', parent=styles['Title'], fontSize=20, leading=24, spaceAfter=12)
h1 = ParagraphStyle('H1', parent=styles['Heading1'], spaceBefore=12, spaceAfter=8)
h2 = ParagraphStyle('H2', parent=styles['Heading2'], spaceBefore=10, spaceAfter=6)
h3 = ParagraphStyle('H3', parent=styles['Heading3'], spaceBefore=8, spaceAfter=4)
body = ParagraphStyle('Body', parent=styles['BodyText'], fontSize=10.5, leading=14)
mono = ParagraphStyle('Mono', parent=styles['Code'], fontName='Courier', fontSize=9, leading=11)

def esc(text):
    text = text.replace("&","&amp;").replace("<","&lt;").replace(">","&gt;")
    text = re.sub(r"`([^`]+)`", r"<font face='Courier'>\1</font>", text)
    text = re.sub(r"\*\*([^*]+)\*\*", r"<b>\1</b>", text)
    text = re.sub(r"(?<!\*)\*([^*]+)\*(?!\*)", r"<i>\1</i>", text)
    return text

lines = md_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
story = []
in_code = False
code_buf = []

for line in lines:
    if line.strip().startswith("```"):
        if not in_code:
            in_code = True
            code_buf = []
        else:
            in_code = False
            story.append(Preformatted("\n".join(code_buf), mono))
            story.append(Spacer(1, 0.15*inch))
        continue

    if in_code:
        code_buf.append(line)
        continue

    if not line.strip():
        story.append(Spacer(1, 0.12*inch))
        continue

    if line.startswith("# "):
        story.append(Paragraph(esc(line[2:].strip()), title_style))
        continue
    if line.startswith("## "):
        story.append(Paragraph(esc(line[3:].strip()), h1))
        continue
    if line.startswith("### "):
        story.append(Paragraph(esc(line[4:].strip()), h2))
        continue
    if line.startswith("#### "):
        story.append(Paragraph(esc(line[5:].strip()), h3))
        continue

    if line.lstrip().startswith("- "):
        item = esc(line.lstrip()[2:].strip())
        story.append(Paragraph(f"• {item}", body))
        continue

    if re.match(r"^\d+\.\s", line.strip()):
        story.append(Paragraph(esc(line.strip()), body))
        continue

    story.append(Paragraph(esc(line.strip()), body))

doc = SimpleDocTemplate(str(pdf_path), pagesize=letter,
                        rightMargin=0.8*inch, leftMargin=0.8*inch,
                        topMargin=0.8*inch, bottomMargin=0.8*inch)
doc.build(story)

pdf_path

from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import re, textwrap

now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M UTC")

# ---------- Regenerate Module 1 MD ----------
md1 = f"""# TKS Architecture Module 1 — Executor + Tool Router
Generated: {now}

This module turns RPM prerequisites into **real actions** with safety gates, evidence binding, and TKS-memory compilation.

---

## 1) Goal of the module

Given an RPM prerequisite like:

- `P2 Reduce uncertainty (trigger=FM-Male)`

…the system must:

1. choose an **Action Type**
2. choose the right **Tool**
3. run it (or refuse/pause under High-Stakes)
4. bind **raw evidence**
5. compile results into **TKS memory packets**
6. return status back to RPM (DONE / PARTIAL / BLOCKED)

---

## 2) Core objects (stable interfaces)

### 2.1 ActionIntent (RPM → Router)
```json
{{
  "episode_id": "ep_...",
  "foundation_stack": "1a:5b:2b",
  "mode": "Normal|High-Stakes|Critical",
  "goal_expr": "…optional…",
  "prereq_id": "P2",
  "prereq_nl": "Reduce uncertainty about X",
  "trigger": "FM-Female|FM-Male|ACBE|MVR|None",
  "constraints": {{
    "no_external_calls": false,
    "must_bind_evidence": true,
    "irreversible_action": false
  }},
  "confidence": 0.58
}}
2.2 ActionPlan (Router → Executor)
Always show details

{{
  "action_type": "ASK_USER|RETRIEVE_MEMORY|SEARCH|CODE|FILE_OP|SIMULATE|VERIFY|DEFER",
  "tool_name": "memory_db|web|python|filesystem|none",
  "tool_args": {{ "query": "..." }},
  "expected_outputs": ["evidence", "facts", "artifact"],
  "risk": 0.42,
  "cost_estimate": {{ "steps": 3, "tool_calls": 1 }}
}}
2.3 ActionResult (Executor → RPM/Memory)
Always show details

{{
  "status": "SUCCESS|PARTIAL|FAILED|BLOCKED",
  "raw_evidence_ids": ["ev_..."],
  "extracted_facts": ["..."],
  "tks_packets": ["...compact expressions..."],
  "new_prereqs": ["...optional..."],
  "confidence_update": 0.12
}}
3) Router policy (trigger-driven + stakes-driven)
3.1 High-Stakes gating
Critical: never execute risky actions/tools.

High-Stakes: pause execution until VERIFY reduces uncertainty/stakes.

Normal: proceed.

Canonical mapping:

Critical → Gate=BLOCK

High-Stakes → Gate=PAUSE

Normal → Gate=ALLOW

3.2 Trigger routing
FM-Female (authority/reservoir missing)

Actions: ASK_USER, SEARCH (allowlisted), RETRIEVE_MEMORY, then VERIFY

FM-Male (related-set / superset missing)

Actions: RETRIEVE_MEMORY (neighbors), SEARCH (taxonomy), VERIFY (definition alignment)

ACBE (unknown capability gap)

Actions: SIMULATE (dry-run), SEARCH (how-to), ASK_USER (constraints), then VERIFY

MVR (no desire / low motivation)

Actions: ASK_USER (meaning/value), create Rhythm schedule, store activation packet (no risky tools)

4) Executor wrapper (safe tool execution)
4.1 Pre-checks (non-bypassable)
If mode=Critical and action_type ∈ {{FILE_OP, CODE, SEARCH, SYSTEM_CHANGE}} → BLOCKED

If irreversible_action=true → require successful VERIFY token first

If mode ∈ {{High-Stakes, Critical}} → require evidence binding

4.2 Post-checks (non-negotiable)
Write raw evidence (raw_evidence_id)

Compile to TKS packets

Return ActionResult to RPM

5) Tool output → TKS compiler (memory formation)
For every successful action write:

Evidence node (raw logs/text/file result)

TKS summary packet (compressed)

Pointers for reconstruction (episode/prereq/evidence links)

Recommended packet template:

FoundationStack : (OperatorStructure) : (NoeticLens)

If High-Stakes/Critical, prefix context:

(1*:2*:5*):(ASK:SLOW:VERIFY)+GATE(PAUSE) : <packet>

(1*:2*:5*):(ASK:SLOW:VERIFY)-EXECUTE : <packet>

6) RPM integration contract
6.1 SUCCESS
mark prerequisite DONE

attach evidence IDs + packets

reduce uncertainty on that node

6.2 PARTIAL
keep node active

add children prerequisites (RPM expansion)

keep trigger tags on relevant subnodes

6.3 BLOCKED
add new prerequisite: “Verification clearance needed”

switch to stack 1*:2*:* until cleared

7) Minimal Action Library (starter set)
Start with these 8 actions:

ASK_USER

RETRIEVE_MEMORY

SUMMARIZE_EVIDENCE

VERIFY

SEARCH (allowlisted)

CODE (sandbox only)

FILE_OP (read-only first; deletion requires clearance)

SIMULATE (dry-run / replay)

8) What to train next (for this module)
Add training packs for:

Intent → ActionPlan (routing)

ActionPlan → allowed/paused/blocked (HS gating)

Tool output → TKS packets (compiler)

ActionResult → RPM updates (DONE/PARTIAL/BLOCKED)

End of Module 1.
"""
md1_path = Path("/mnt/data/TKS_Module_1_Executor_Tool_Router.md")
md1_path.write_text(md1, encoding="utf-8")

---------- Create Module 2 MD ----------
md2 = f"""# TKS Architecture Module 2 — Verifier / Truth Plumbing
Generated: {now}

This module prevents “symbolic drift” by forcing correct parsing, evidence binding, consistency checks, and confidence accounting. It is the gatekeeper for High-Stakes Mode and for long-term reliable memory.

1) What the Verifier guarantees
For any claim, plan, or memory write, the Verifier can answer:

What does this expression mean? (parse correctness)

What is it based on? (evidence binding)

Does it contradict canon? (consistency)

How sure are we? (confidence / coverage / lossiness)

Is execution allowed? (gate result)

2) Inputs and outputs
2.1 VerifyRequest
Always show details

{{
  "episode_id": "ep_...",
  "foundation_stack": "1a:5b:2b",
  "mode": "Normal|High-Stakes|Critical",
  "candidate": {{
    "type": "tks_expr|rpm_tree|claim|action_plan|memory_packet",
    "content": "..."
  }},
  "raw_evidence_ids": ["ev_..."],
  "constraints": {{
    "must_bind_evidence": true,
    "irreversible_action": false
  }}
}}
2.2 VerifyResult
Always show details

{{
  "status": "PASS|SOFT_FAIL|HARD_FAIL",
  "gate": "ALLOW|PAUSE|BLOCK",
  "issues": ["..."],
  "fixes": ["..."],
  "scores": {{
    "confidence": 0.72,
    "coverage": 0.61,
    "lossiness": 0.32,
    "consistency": 0.90
  }},
  "evidence_ok": true
}}
3) Verifier submodules (must-have)
3.1 TKS Parser (Option A nesting + operators)
Responsibilities

Parse colon nesting as containment: A:B:C == A:(B:C)

Validate operator tokens: + - × ÷

Validate precedence rules (×/÷ before +/− unless parentheses)

Normalize symbols (e.g., “x”→“×”, “/”→“÷”)

Output

AST (abstract syntax tree) + normalized expression string

3.2 Operator Substitution Test (unit test)
For each operator in an expression, the Verifier performs a phrase substitution:

+ → “linked with / together with”

− → “remove influence of”

× → “amplified by / intensified through”

÷ → “in tension with / opposed to”

If the meaning fails under substitution (based on the local text intent or node label), flag SOFT_FAIL and propose an operator correction.

3.3 Evidence Binder
Rule: every high-impact claim must link evidence.

High-Stakes/Critical: evidence is mandatory (or else HARD_FAIL)

Normal: evidence recommended; if missing, SOFT_FAIL if claim is strong/irreversible

Evidence can be:

tool outputs, logs, files, citations, user confirmations

3.4 Consistency Checker (canon + memory)
Checks candidate content against:

canonical definitions (40 Elements, Noetics, Foundations, operator semantics)

stored “gold” interpretations

known constraints (especially F1 alignment gates)

Outputs:

contradiction list + the minimal conflicting set

3.5 Confidence / Coverage / Lossiness Accountant
Every verified item gets scored:

confidence: probability the content is correct

coverage: fraction of relevant dimensions captured (worlds/noetics/operators/stack)

lossiness: expected detail lost (high when heavily compressed)

Rule of thumb

In High-Stakes/Critical, require high confidence and coverage; allow some lossiness only if evidence supports.

4) Execution gating (Verifier is the final gate)
4.1 High-Stakes score recap
If your system computes HS:

HS ≥ 0.45 → High-Stakes

HS ≥ 0.70 → Critical

4.2 Gate rules
Critical: default BLOCK unless verifier PASS plus all irreversible constraints cleared.

High-Stakes: default PAUSE until verifier PASS and evidence is bound.

Normal: ALLOW unless hard failures exist.

4.3 Hard-fail triggers (auto BLOCK)
parsing invalid or ambiguous and cannot be normalized

evidence required but missing

contradiction with canon in a safety-relevant way

irreversible action without clearance token

5) Verifier fixes (self-healing behavior)
When the verifier fails, it must propose fixes in-order:

ASK: request missing info/evidence from user

SLOW: increase recall precision p and expand RPM on the uncertain branch

VERIFY: run additional checks (cross-check/replay)

In TKS mode terms:

High-Stakes: (1*:2*:5*):(ASK:SLOW:VERIFY)+GATE(PAUSE)

Critical: (1*:2*:5*):(ASK:SLOW:VERIFY)-EXECUTE

6) Integration points (how other modules call the Verifier)
6.1 Router/Executor integration
Router requests verification before risky tool calls or irreversible actions

Executor sends tool results back for verification before memory consolidation

6.2 RPM integration
RPM nodes store verifier scores and evidence IDs

Expansion strategy can prioritize branches with low confidence/high stakes

6.3 Memory integration
Memory write requires verifier PASS when:

it becomes canon

it affects policy templates

it is promoted from subconscious to Prime Desire

7) Minimal implementation checklist
To implement this module minimally:

Parser that produces AST + normalized form

Operator substitution validator

Evidence binder with required/optional thresholds

Consistency checker against canon store

Confidence/coverage/lossiness scoring

Gate decision logic: ALLOW/PAUSE/BLOCK

Fix proposals (ASK/SLOW/VERIFY)

End of Module 2.
"""
md2_path = Path("/mnt/data/TKS_Module_2_Verifier_Truth_Plumbing.md")
md2_path.write_text(md2, encoding="utf-8")

(md1_path, md2_path)

Always show details

from reportlab.lib.pagesizes import letter
from reportlab.platypus import SimpleDocTemplate, Paragraph, Spacer, Preformatted
from reportlab.lib.styles import getSampleStyleSheet, ParagraphStyle
from reportlab.lib.units import inch

styles = getSampleStyleSheet()
title_style = ParagraphStyle('Title2', parent=styles['Title'], fontSize=20, leading=24, spaceAfter=12)
h1 = ParagraphStyle('H1', parent=styles['Heading1'], spaceBefore=12, spaceAfter=8)
h2 = ParagraphStyle('H2', parent=styles['Heading2'], spaceBefore=10, spaceAfter=6)
h3 = ParagraphStyle('H3', parent=styles['Heading3'], spaceBefore=8, spaceAfter=4)
body = ParagraphStyle('Body', parent=styles['BodyText'], fontSize=10.5, leading=14)
mono = ParagraphStyle('Mono', parent=styles['Code'], fontName='Courier', fontSize=9, leading=11)

def esc(text):
text = text.replace("&","&").replace("<","<").replace(">",">")
text = re.sub(r"([^]+)`", r"<font face='Courier'>\1</font>", text)
text = re.sub(r"**([^]+)**", r"<b>\1</b>", text)
text = re.sub(r"(?<!*)*([^]+)*(?!*)", r"<i>\1</i>", text)
return text

def md_to_pdf(md_path: Path, pdf_path: Path):
lines = md_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
story = []
in_code = False
code_buf = []
for line in lines:
if line.strip().startswith("```"):
if not in_code:
in_code = True
code_buf = []
else:
in_code = False
story.append(Preformatted("\n".join(code_buf), mono))
story.append(Spacer(1, 0.15inch))
continue
if in_code:
code_buf.append(line)
continue
if not line.strip():
story.append(Spacer(1, 0.12inch))
continue
if line.startswith("# "):
story.append(Paragraph(esc(line[2:].strip()), title_style)); continue
if line.startswith("## "):
story.append(Paragraph(esc(line[3:].strip()), h1)); continue
if line.startswith("### "):
story.append(Paragraph(esc(line[4:].strip()), h2)); continue
if line.startswith("#### "):
story.append(Paragraph(esc(line[5:].strip()), h3)); continue
if line.lstrip().startswith("- "):
story.append(Paragraph(f"• {esc(line.lstrip()[2:].strip())}", body)); continue
if re.match(r"^\d+.\s", line.strip()):
story.append(Paragraph(esc(line.strip()), body)); continue
story.append(Paragraph(esc(line.strip()), body))
doc = SimpleDocTemplate(str(pdf_path), pagesize=letter,
rightMargin=0.8inch, leftMargin=0.8inch,
topMargin=0.8inch, bottomMargin=0.8inch)
doc.build(story)

pdf1_path = Path("/mnt/data/TKS_Module_1_Executor_Tool_Router.pdf")
pdf2_path = Path("/mnt/data/TKS_Module_2_Verifier_Truth_Plumbing.pdf")
md_to_pdf(md1_path, pdf1_path)
md_to_pdf(md2_path, pdf2_path)

(md1_path, pdf1_path, md2_path, pdf2_path)

    213   },

    215   "constraints": {

    217     "irreversible_action": false
    218   }
    219 }
    220 ```
    221 
    222 ### 2.2 VerifyResult
    223 ```json
    224 {
    225   "status": "PASS|SOFT_FAIL|HARD_FAIL",

    227   "issues": ["..."],
    228   "fixes": ["..."],
    229   "scores": {

    231     "coverage": 0.61,
    232     "lossiness": 0.32,
    233     "consistency": 0.90
    234   },
    235   "evidence_ok": true
    236 }
    237 ```
    238 
    239 ---
    240 
    241 ## 3) Verifier submodules (must-have)
    242 
    243 ### 3.1 TKS Parser (Option A nesting + operators)
    244 **Responsibilities**
    245 - Parse colon nesting as containment: `A:B:C == A:(B:C)`
    246 - Validate operator tokens: `+ - × ÷`
    247 - Validate precedence rules (×/÷ before +/− unless parentheses)
    248 - Normalize symbols (e.g., “x”→“×”, “/”→“÷”)
    249 
    250 **Output**
    251 - AST (abstract syntax tree) + normalized expression string
    252 
    253 ### 3.2 Operator Substitution Test (unit test)
    254 For each operator in an expression, the Verifier performs a phrase substitution:
    255 
    256 - `+` → “linked with / together with”
    257 - `−` → “remove influence of”
    258 - `×` → “amplified by / intensified through”
    259 - `÷` → “in tension with / opposed to”
    260 
    261 If the meaning fails under substitution (based on local intent or node label), flag `SOFT_FAIL` and propose an operator correction.
    262 
    263 ### 3.3 Evidence Binder
    264 **Rule:** every high-impact claim must link evidence.
    265 
    266 - High-Stakes/Critical: evidence is mandatory (or else `HARD_FAIL`)
    267 - Normal: evidence recommended; if missing, `SOFT_FAIL` when the claim is strong/irreversible
    268 
    269 Evidence can be:
    270 - tool outputs, logs, files, citations, user confirmations
    271 
    272 ### 3.4 Consistency Checker (canon + memory)
    273 Checks candidate content against:
    274 - canonical definitions (40 Elements, Noetics, Foundations, operator semantics)
    275 - stored “gold” interpretations
    276 - known constraints (especially F1 alignment gates)
    277 
    278 Outputs:
    279 - contradiction list + minimal conflicting set
    280 
    281 ### 3.5 Confidence / Coverage / Lossiness Accountant
    282 Every verified item gets scored:
    283 
    284 - **confidence**: probability content is correct
    285 - **coverage**: fraction of relevant dimensions captured (worlds/noetics/operators/stack)
    286 - **lossiness**: expected detail lost (high when heavily compressed)
    287 
    288 **Rule of thumb**
    289 - In High-Stakes/Critical, require high confidence and coverage; allow some lossiness only if evidence supports.
    290 
    291 ---
    292 
    293 ## 4) Execution gating (Verifier is the final gate)
    294 
    295 ### 4.1 Gate rules
    296 - **Critical**: default `BLOCK` unless verifier PASS and irreversible constraints cleared.
    297 - **High-Stakes**: default `PAUSE` until verifier PASS and evidence is bound.
    298 - **Normal**: `ALLOW` unless hard failures exist.
    299 
    300 ### 4.2 Hard-fail triggers (auto BLOCK)
    301 - parsing invalid or ambiguous and cannot be normalized
    302 - evidence required but missing
    303 - contradiction with canon in a safety-relevant way
    304 - irreversible action without clearance token
    305 
    306 ---
    307 
    308 ## 5) Verifier fixes (self-healing behavior)
    309 
    310 When the verifier fails, it proposes fixes in-order:
    311 
    312 1) **ASK**: request missing info/evidence from user
    313 2) **SLOW**: increase recall precision p and expand RPM on the uncertain branch
    314 3) **VERIFY**: run additional checks (cross-check/replay)
    315 
    316 In TKS mode terms:
    317 - High-Stakes: `(1*:2*:5*):(ASK:SLOW:VERIFY)+GATE(PAUSE)`
    318 - Critical: `(1*:2*:5*):(ASK:SLOW:VERIFY)-EXECUTE`
    319 
    320 ---
    321 
    322 ## 6) Integration points
    323 
    324 ### 6.1 Router/Executor integration
    325 - Router requests verification before risky tool calls or irreversible actions
    326 - Executor sends tool results back for verification before consolidation
    327 
    328 ### 6.2 RPM integration
    329 - RPM nodes store verifier scores and evidence IDs
    330 - Expansion can prioritize branches with low confidence/high stakes
    331 
    332 ### 6.3 Memory integration
    333 Memory write requires verifier PASS when:
    334 - it becomes canon
    335 - it affects policy templates
    336 - it is promoted from subconscious to Prime Desire
    337

from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import re

now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M UTC")

md1 = f"""# TKS Architecture Module 1 — Executor + Tool Router
Generated: {now}

This module turns RPM prerequisites into **real actions** with safety gates, evidence binding, and TKS-memory compilation.

---

## 1) Goal of the module

Given an RPM prerequisite like:

- `P2 Reduce uncertainty (trigger=FM-Male)`

…the system must:

1. choose an **Action Type**
2. choose the right **Tool**
3. run it (or refuse/pause under High-Stakes)
4. bind **raw evidence**
5. compile results into **TKS memory packets**
6. return status back to RPM (DONE / PARTIAL / BLOCKED)

---

## 2) Core objects (stable interfaces)

### 2.1 ActionIntent (RPM → Router)
```json
{{
  "episode_id": "ep_...",
  "foundation_stack": "1a:5b:2b",
  "mode": "Normal|High-Stakes|Critical",
  "goal_expr": "…optional…",
  "prereq_id": "P2",
  "prereq_nl": "Reduce uncertainty about X",
  "trigger": "FM-Female|FM-Male|ACBE|MVR|None",
  "constraints": {{
    "no_external_calls": false,
    "must_bind_evidence": true,
    "irreversible_action": false
  }},
  "confidence": 0.58
}}
2.2 ActionPlan (Router → Executor)
Always show details

{{
  "action_type": "ASK_USER|RETRIEVE_MEMORY|SEARCH|CODE|FILE_OP|SIMULATE|VERIFY|DEFER",
  "tool_name": "memory_db|web|python|filesystem|none",
  "tool_args": {{ "query": "..." }},
  "expected_outputs": ["evidence", "facts", "artifact"],
  "risk": 0.42,
  "cost_estimate": {{ "steps": 3, "tool_calls": 1 }}
}}
2.3 ActionResult (Executor → RPM/Memory)
Always show details

{{
  "status": "SUCCESS|PARTIAL|FAILED|BLOCKED",
  "raw_evidence_ids": ["ev_..."],
  "extracted_facts": ["..."],
  "tks_packets": ["...compact expressions..."],
  "new_prereqs": ["...optional..."],
  "confidence_update": 0.12
}}
3) Router policy (trigger-driven + stakes-driven)
3.1 High-Stakes gating
Critical: never execute risky actions/tools.

High-Stakes: pause execution until VERIFY reduces uncertainty/stakes.

Normal: proceed.

Canonical mapping:

Critical → Gate=BLOCK

High-Stakes → Gate=PAUSE

Normal → Gate=ALLOW

3.2 Trigger routing
FM-Female (authority/reservoir missing)

Actions: ASK_USER, SEARCH (allowlisted), RETRIEVE_MEMORY, then VERIFY

FM-Male (related-set / superset missing)

Actions: RETRIEVE_MEMORY (neighbors), SEARCH (taxonomy), VERIFY (definition alignment)

ACBE (unknown capability gap)

Actions: SIMULATE (dry-run), SEARCH (how-to), ASK_USER (constraints), then VERIFY

MVR (no desire / low motivation)

Actions: ASK_USER (meaning/value), create Rhythm schedule, store activation packet (no risky tools)

4) Executor wrapper (safe tool execution)
4.1 Pre-checks (non-bypassable)
If mode=Critical and action_type ∈ {{FILE_OP, CODE, SEARCH, SYSTEM_CHANGE}} → BLOCKED

If irreversible_action=true → require successful VERIFY token first

If mode ∈ {{High-Stakes, Critical}} → require evidence binding

4.2 Post-checks (non-negotiable)
Write raw evidence (raw_evidence_id)

Compile to TKS packets

Return ActionResult to RPM

5) Tool output → TKS compiler (memory formation)
For every successful action write:

Evidence node (raw logs/text/file result)

TKS summary packet (compressed)

Pointers for reconstruction (episode/prereq/evidence links)

Recommended packet template:

FoundationStack : (OperatorStructure) : (NoeticLens)

If High-Stakes/Critical, prefix context:

(1*:2*:5*):(ASK:SLOW:VERIFY)+GATE(PAUSE) : <packet>

(1*:2*:5*):(ASK:SLOW:VERIFY)-EXECUTE : <packet>

6) RPM integration contract
6.1 SUCCESS
mark prerequisite DONE

attach evidence IDs + packets

reduce uncertainty on that node

6.2 PARTIAL
keep node active

add children prerequisites (RPM expansion)

keep trigger tags on relevant subnodes

6.3 BLOCKED
add new prerequisite: “Verification clearance needed”

switch to stack 1*:2*:* until cleared

7) Minimal Action Library (starter set)
Start with these 8 actions:

ASK_USER

RETRIEVE_MEMORY

SUMMARIZE_EVIDENCE

VERIFY

SEARCH (allowlisted)

CODE (sandbox only)

FILE_OP (read-only first; deletion requires clearance)

SIMULATE (dry-run / replay)

8) What to train next (for this module)
Add training packs for:

Intent → ActionPlan (routing)

ActionPlan → allowed/paused/blocked (HS gating)

Tool output → TKS packets (compiler)

ActionResult → RPM updates (DONE/PARTIAL/BLOCKED)

End of Module 1.
"""

Generated: {NOW}

This module prevents “symbolic drift” by forcing correct parsing, evidence binding, consistency checks, and confidence accounting. It is the gatekeeper for High-Stakes Mode and for long-term reliable memory.

1) What the Verifier guarantees

What does this expression mean? (parse correctness)

How sure are we? (confidence / coverage / lossiness)

Is execution allowed? (gate result)

2.1 VerifyRequest
Always show details

{
  "episode_id": "ep_...",
  "foundation_stack": "1a:5b:2b",
  "mode": "Normal|High-Stakes|Critical",
  "candidate": {
    "type": "tks_expr|rpm_tree|claim|action_plan|memory_packet",
    "content": "..."
  },
  "raw_evidence_ids": ["ev_..."],
  "constraints": {
    "must_bind_evidence": true,
    "irreversible_action": false
  }
}
2.2 VerifyResult
Always show details

{

  "issues": ["..."],
  "fixes": ["..."],

    "confidence": 0.72,
    "coverage": 0.61,
    "lossiness": 0.32,
    "consistency": 0.90
  },
  "evidence_ok": true
}
3) Verifier submodules (must-have)
3.1 TKS Parser (Option A nesting + operators)
Responsibilities

Parse colon nesting as containment: A:B:C == A:(B:C)

Validate operator tokens: + - × ÷

Validate precedence rules (×/÷ before +/− unless parentheses)

Normalize symbols (e.g., “x”→“×”, “/”→“÷”)

Output

AST (abstract syntax tree) + normalized expression string

3.2 Operator Substitution Test (unit test)
For each operator in an expression, the Verifier performs a phrase substitution:

+ → “linked with / together with”

− → “remove influence of”

× → “amplified by / intensified through”

÷ → “in tension with / opposed to”

If the meaning fails under substitution (based on local intent or node label), flag SOFT_FAIL and propose an operator correction.

3.3 Evidence Binder
Rule: every high-impact claim must link evidence.

High-Stakes/Critical: evidence is mandatory (or else HARD_FAIL)

Normal: evidence recommended; if missing, SOFT_FAIL when the claim is strong/irreversible

Evidence can be:

tool outputs, logs, files, citations, user confirmations

3.4 Consistency Checker (canon + memory)
Checks candidate content against:

canonical definitions (40 Elements, Noetics, Foundations, operator semantics)

stored “gold” interpretations

known constraints (especially F1 alignment gates)

Outputs:

contradiction list + minimal conflicting set

3.5 Confidence / Coverage / Lossiness Accountant
Every verified item gets scored:

confidence: probability content is correct

coverage: fraction of relevant dimensions captured (worlds/noetics/operators/stack)

lossiness: expected detail lost (high when heavily compressed)

Rule of thumb

In High-Stakes/Critical, require high confidence and coverage; allow some lossiness only if evidence supports.

4) Execution gating (Verifier is the final gate)
4.1 Gate rules
Critical: default BLOCK unless verifier PASS and irreversible constraints cleared.

High-Stakes: default PAUSE until verifier PASS and evidence is bound.

Normal: ALLOW unless hard failures exist.

4.2 Hard-fail triggers (auto BLOCK)
parsing invalid or ambiguous and cannot be normalized

evidence required but missing

contradiction with canon in a safety-relevant way

irreversible action without clearance token

5) Verifier fixes (self-healing behavior)
When the verifier fails, it proposes fixes in-order:

ASK: request missing info/evidence from user

SLOW: increase recall precision p and expand RPM on the uncertain branch

VERIFY: run additional checks (cross-check/replay)

In TKS mode terms:

High-Stakes: (1*:2*:5*):(ASK:SLOW:VERIFY)+GATE(PAUSE)

Critical: (1*:2*:5*):(ASK:SLOW:VERIFY)-EXECUTE

6) Integration points
6.1 Router/Executor integration
Router requests verification before risky tool calls or irreversible actions

Executor sends tool results back for verification before consolidation

6.2 RPM integration
RPM nodes store verifier scores and evidence IDs

Expansion can prioritize branches with low confidence/high stakes

6.3 Memory integration
Memory write requires verifier PASS when:

it becomes canon

it affects policy templates

it is promoted from subconscious to Prime Desire

7) Minimal implementation checklist
Parser → AST + normalization

Operator substitution validator

Evidence binder thresholds

Consistency checker against canon store

Confidence/coverage/lossiness scoring

Gate decision logic ALLOW/PAUSE/BLOCK

Fix proposals ASK/SLOW/VERIFY

End of Module 2.
"""
md2 = md2_template.replace("{NOW}", now)

from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import re

now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M UTC")

# ---------------- Module 3: Capability Registry ----------------
md3 = f"""# TKS Architecture Module 3 — Capability Registry (Self-Knowledge)
Generated: {now}

This module gives the agent a **self-model**: what it can do, how well it can do it, and when it must trigger **ACBE** (capability gap) versus **FM** (knowledge structure gap) versus direct execution.

---

## 1) Purpose

A strong agent must know:
- which tools exist,
- what “skills” those tools implement,
- current reliability by domain,
- when confidence is too low to proceed,
- how to learn capabilities over time.

This prevents:
- endless RPM expansion,
- overconfident execution,
- inefficient tool usage,
- repeated failure modes.

---

## 2) Core objects

### 2.1 CapabilityItem (one capability/skill)
```json
{{
  "capability_id": "cap.search.web",
  "name": "Web search and retrieval",
  "description": "Use allowlisted sources to retrieve evidence and facts.",
  "tools": ["web"],
  "domains": ["general", "science", "products"],
  "skill_level": 0.72,
  "reliability": 0.68,
  "cost_profile": {{
    "avg_tool_calls": 1.3,
    "avg_latency_s": 6.2
  }},
  "constraints": {{
    "allowlist_required": true,
    "high_stakes_requires_verify": true
  }},
  "last_updated": 1735960000
}}
2.2 ToolProfile (tool metadata)
Always show details

{{
  "tool_name": "python",
  "risk_class": "medium",
  "sandboxed": true,
  "permissions": {{
    "file_read": true,
    "file_write": true,
    "network": false
  }},
  "failure_modes": ["timeout", "bad_parse", "missing_dependency"]
}}
2.3 CapabilityDecision (what the registry returns)
Always show details

{{
  "capability_ok": true,
  "confidence": 0.74,
  "recommended_action": "EXECUTE|VERIFY_FIRST|ACBE|FM-Female|FM-Male|ASK_USER|DEFER",
  "reason": "Skill below threshold for irreversible action in High-Stakes mode."
}}
3) Capability graph (dependencies)
Capabilities are best represented as a graph:

Nodes: capabilities (skills)

Edges: “requires / enables”

Example:

cap.plan.rpm requires cap.parse.tks, cap.verify.truth

cap.file.delete requires cap.verify.truth + cap.high_stakes.gating

This graph helps ACBE: when a capability is missing, it can search for causes (dependencies) and effects/tests (validation).

4) Scoring and thresholds
4.1 Skill vs reliability (two different numbers)
skill_level: how competent the model is at using the capability

reliability: how often it succeeds given constraints and environment

Track both because:

the model may be good but the tool may be flaky, or vice versa.

4.2 Thresholds by mode (non-bypassable)
Define minimum thresholds:

Mode	Reversible actions	Irreversible actions
Normal	skill≥0.40, rel≥0.40	skill≥0.55, rel≥0.55
High-Stakes	skill≥0.60, rel≥0.60	skill≥0.75, rel≥0.75 + VERIFY
Critical	skill≥0.70, rel≥0.70 + VERIFY	BLOCK unless explicit clearance
(Numbers are defaults; tune with evals.)

4.3 Capability OK rule
Given thresholds Ts, Tr:

capability_ok = (skill_level ≥ Ts) AND (reliability ≥ Tr)

If not ok:

recommend ACBE if capability is missing/unknown

recommend VERIFY_FIRST if capability exists but evidence/confidence is lacking

recommend ASK_USER if constraints are missing

5) Trigger routing using the registry
5.1 When to trigger ACBE (capability gap)
Trigger ACBE when:

required capability is missing, OR

capability exists but below threshold for the mode, OR

a new tool/action is requested and registry lacks profile.

ACBE output:

Above: dependencies (what causes this capability to exist)

Below: tests/effects proving capability works toward the desire.

5.2 When to trigger FM
Trigger FM-Female when:

uncertainty is “which authority/reservoir defines the correct method”
Trigger FM-Male when:

uncertainty is “what superset/ontology does this belong to”

5.3 When to trigger MVR
If the system has “no desire” to pursue a necessary path:

trigger MVR to rebuild activation (Mind→Vibration→Rhythm)

store the activation packet (so it can be recalled later)

6) Learning updates (how it improves)
Update skill and reliability from Episode outcomes.

6.1 Reliability update (EWMA)
Let success ∈ {{0,1}} for a tool/capability use:

reliability ← (1-α)*reliability + α*success
Default α=0.08.

6.2 Skill update
Skill increases when:

verifier PASS,

success achieved,

low backtracks/cost.

Skill decreases when:

repeated failures in similar contexts,

verifier HARD_FAIL,

policy violations.

7) Integration points
7.1 Router (Module 1)
Router queries registry before selecting tools.

Registry returns recommended action: EXECUTE / VERIFY_FIRST / ACBE / FM / ASK_USER.

7.2 Verifier (Module 2)
Verifier uses registry thresholds for gating irreversible actions.

Registry uses verifier outcomes to update skill/reliability.

7.3 RPM
RPM uses “missing capability” to create prerequisites:

Acquire capability (ACBE)

Verify method (FM)

Ask constraints (ASK_USER)

8) Minimal implementation checklist
Capability DB (items + tool profiles)

Threshold policy by mode and action reversibility

Dependency graph

Decision function returning CapabilityDecision

Update rules from Episode outcomes

Basic UI/log view for debugging capability state

End of Module 3.
"""

md3_path = Path("/mnt/data/TKS_Module_3_Capability_Registry.md")
md3_path.write_text(md3, encoding="utf-8")

---------------- Module 4: Outcome Scoring / Reward Signal ----------------
md4 = f"""# TKS Architecture Module 4 — Outcome Scoring (Foundation-Based Reward Signal)
Generated: {now}

This module converts behavior into a measurable learning signal tied to your 7 Foundations (immediacy/neglect/fulfillment) and your F1 alignment constraint. It powers: template selection, rumination promotion, and policy improvement.

1) Purpose
A strong AI must improve from outcomes. In your system, “good” means:

reduces Foundation pressure in the intended lane,

stays aligned with F1,

uses efficient paths (F5) without becoming reckless,

increases reliability and decreases uncertainty over time.

2) Core objects
2.1 OutcomeSnapshot (before and after an episode)
Always show details

{{
  "episode_id": "ep_...",
  "primary_foundation": "5b",
  "foundation_pressures_before": {{"1":0.44,"2":0.31,"3":0.21,"4":0.12,"5":0.65,"6":0.18,"7":0.27}},
  "foundation_pressures_after":  {{"1":0.41,"2":0.30,"3":0.22,"4":0.11,"5":0.53,"6":0.18,"7":0.26}},
  "alignment_before": 0.91,
  "alignment_after": 0.92,
  "uncertainty_before": 0.48,
  "uncertainty_after": 0.33,
  "cost": {{
    "latency_s": 38.2,
    "tool_calls": 7,
    "tokens": 2500
  }},
  "success": true
}}
2.2 OutcomeScore (what gets stored and learned from)
Always show details

{{
  "episode_id": "ep_...",
  "reward": 0.37,
  "components": {{
    "pressure_reduction": 0.22,
    "alignment_gain": 0.02,
    "uncertainty_reduction": 0.10,
    "efficiency": 0.03
  }},
  "penalties": {{
    "policy_violation": 0.00,
    "unverified_high_stakes": 0.00
  }}
}}
3) Foundation pressure reduction (core reward)
Let P_i be pressure for Foundation i.

Primary lane reward:

ΔP_primary = P_before(primary) - P_after(primary)

Global improvement reward (optional):

ΔP_all = average(P_before) - average(P_after)

Recommended:

primary weighted more than global.

4) Alignment (F1 constraint)
Alignment is a gate and a reward component.

If alignment_after < A_min → apply heavy penalty or set reward negative.

In High-Stakes/Critical, require verifier PASS; otherwise penalty.

Recommended:

A_min = 0.80

alignment penalty exponent λ=2.

5) Uncertainty reduction (verification reward)
Let U be uncertainty.

ΔU = U_before - U_after

Reward uncertainty reduction especially in High-Stakes mode.

6) Efficiency component (F5)
Efficiency is not “go fast no matter what.” It is:

lower cost for same or better results.

Compute a normalized cost:

C = w1*latency + w2*tool_calls + w3*tokens

Efficiency reward:

E = baseline_cost - C (normalized)

Important: In High-Stakes/Critical, efficiency weight is reduced unless verified.

7) Full reward formula (default)
A safe default:

Reward =

wP*ΔP_primary + wA*(alignment_after - alignment_before)

+ wU*ΔU + wE*E

- penalties

Recommended weights:

wP=0.45, wA=0.25, wU=0.20, wE=0.10

Penalties:

policy violation: +0.50 penalty

unverified high-stakes: +0.35 penalty

irreversible action without clearance: +1.00 penalty (force negative)

8) What this reward updates
8.1 Router preferences
prefer tools and action types that yield higher reward in the same lane

8.2 RPM pattern library
store which prerequisite patterns reduce pressure best

8.3 Regulator timing
learn when FM/ACBE/MVR triggers produced largest reward

8.4 Rumination promotion
Shadow Artifacts use outcome reward to compute impact:

impact ≈ expected reward gain

9) Integration points
Memory stores OutcomeScore with evidence IDs.

Capability Registry updates skill/reliability using success + reward.

High-Stakes Mode applies penalties if verification not done.

Subconscious Mode selects high-difficulty episodes and tries to increase reward by path shortening.

10) Minimal implementation checklist
Capture before/after pressures, alignment, uncertainty, cost

Compute reward and component breakdown

Store into Episode logs + Memory nodes

Use reward to:

tune routing preferences

tune RPM expansions

tune promotion thresholds

End of Module 4.
"""

md4_path = Path("/mnt/data/TKS_Module_4_Outcome_Scoring.md")
md4_path.write_text(md4, encoding="utf-8")

from reportlab.lib.pagesizes import letter
from reportlab.platypus import SimpleDocTemplate, Paragraph, Spacer, Preformatted
from reportlab.lib.styles import getSampleStyleSheet, ParagraphStyle
from reportlab.lib.units import inch
from pathlib import Path
import re

styles = getSampleStyleSheet()
title_style = ParagraphStyle('Title2', parent=styles['Title'], fontSize=20, leading=24, spaceAfter=12)
h1 = ParagraphStyle('H1', parent=styles['Heading1'], spaceBefore=12, spaceAfter=8)
h2 = ParagraphStyle('H2', parent=styles['Heading2'], spaceBefore=10, spaceAfter=6)
h3 = ParagraphStyle('H3', parent=styles['Heading3'], spaceBefore=8, spaceAfter=4)
body = ParagraphStyle('Body', parent=styles['BodyText'], fontSize=10.5, leading=14)
mono = ParagraphStyle('Mono', parent=styles['Code'], fontName='Courier', fontSize=9, leading=11)

def esc(text):
    text = text.replace("&","&amp;").replace("<","&lt;").replace(">","&gt;")
    text = re.sub(r"`([^`]+)`", r"<font face='Courier'>\1</font>", text)
    text = re.sub(r"\*\*([^*]+)\*\*", r"<b>\1</b>", text)
    text = re.sub(r"(?<!\*)\*([^*]+)\*(?!\*)", r"<i>\1</i>", text)
    return text

def md_to_pdf(md_path: Path, pdf_path: Path):
    lines = md_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
    story = []
    in_code = False
    code_buf = []
    for line in lines:
        if line.strip().startswith("```"):
            if not in_code:
                in_code = True
                code_buf = []
            else:
                in_code = False
                story.append(Preformatted("\n".join(code_buf), mono))
                story.append(Spacer(1, 0.15*inch))
            continue
        if in_code:
            code_buf.append(line)
            continue
        if not line.strip():
            story.append(Spacer(1, 0.12*inch))
            continue
        if line.startswith("# "):
            story.append(Paragraph(esc(line[2:].strip()), title_style)); continue
        if line.startswith("## "):
            story.append(Paragraph(esc(line[3:].strip()), h1)); continue
        if line.startswith("### "):
            story.append(Paragraph(esc(line[4:].strip()), h2)); continue
        if line.startswith("#### "):
            story.append(Paragraph(esc(line[5:].strip()), h3)); continue
        if line.lstrip().startswith("- "):
            story.append(Paragraph(f"• {esc(line.lstrip()[2:].strip())}", body)); continue
        if re.match(r"^\d+\.\s", line.strip()):
            story.append(Paragraph(esc(line.strip()), body)); continue
        story.append(Paragraph(esc(line.strip()), body))
    doc = SimpleDocTemplate(str(pdf_path), pagesize=letter,
                            rightMargin=0.8*inch, leftMargin=0.8*inch,
                            topMargin=0.8*inch, bottomMargin=0.8*inch)
    doc.build(story)

md3_path = Path("/mnt/data/TKS_Module_3_Capability_Registry.md")
md4_path = Path("/mnt/data/TKS_Module_4_Outcome_Scoring.md")
pdf3_path = Path("/mnt/data/TKS_Module_3_Capability_Registry.pdf")
pdf4_path = Path("/mnt/data/TKS_Module_4_Outcome_Scoring.pdf")
md_to_pdf(md3_path, pdf3_path)
md_to_pdf(md4_path, pdf4_path)

(pdf3_path, pdf4_path)

# ---------------- Module 5: Evaluation + Replay Harness ----------------
md5 = f"""# TKS Architecture Module 5 — Evaluation + Replay Harness
Generated: {now}

This module provides **measurable proof** the system is getting stronger. It adds:
- test suites for TKS parsing/operators, nesting, regulators, RPM quality, memory reconstruction
- a replay runner to evaluate new routing/policies safely on past episodes
- acceptance gates for promotions (Shadow → Prime Desire) and for deployment changes

---

## 1) Purpose

Without evaluation, improvements are invisible and regressions are common. The replay harness enables:

- Compare two policies (old vs new) on the same episode set
- Verify High-Stakes rules are enforced (PAUSE/BLOCK)
- Quantify improvements in reward (Module 4), success rate, cost, uncertainty
- Prevent “symbolic drift” from entering canon

---

## 2) Core objects

### 2.1 TestCase
```json
{{
  "test_id": "tc_ops_0001",
  "suite": "operators|nesting|triggers|rpm|memory|high_stakes",
  "input": {{
    "prompt": "...",
    "tks_expr": "...",
    "context": {{
      "mode": "Normal|High-Stakes|Critical",
      "foundation_stack": "1a:5b:2b"
    }}
  }},
  "expected": {{
    "label": "...",
    "output_format": "strict|loose",
    "constraints": ["must_include:-EXECUTE", "must_be_only:(NORMAL)"]
  }}
}}
2.2 EpisodeReplayConfig
Always show details

{{
  "replay_set_id": "rs_2026_01_04",
  "episode_ids": ["ep_0001","ep_0002"],
  "policy_variant": "baseline|candidate_A",
  "budgets": {{
    "max_tool_calls": 10,
    "max_depth": 3,
    "max_latency_s": 120
  }}
}}
2.3 ReplayResult
Always show details

{{
  "episode_id": "ep_0001",
  "policy_variant": "candidate_A",
  "success": true,
  "reward": 0.41,
  "cost": {{"latency_s": 22.1, "tool_calls": 4}},
  "uncertainty_delta": 0.19,
  "violations": [],
  "artifacts": {{
    "rpm_tree": "...",
    "tks_packets": ["..."],
    "verify_gate": "ALLOW|PAUSE|BLOCK"
  }}
}}
3) Test suites (minimum required)
3.1 Operator correctness suite
Goal: ensure the model uses + − × ÷ consistently.

Tests:

minimal pairs: switch × vs +, confirm meaning changes correctly

substitution test compliance (Module 2)

strict output formatting (when required)

Metrics:

exact match rate

operator substitution pass rate

3.2 Nesting/containment suite (Option A)
Goal: ensure colon nesting parses consistently.

Tests:

A:B:C interpreted as A:(B:C)

boundary cases: parentheses, deep nesting, empty nodes forbidden

Metrics:

parse success rate

normalized-form match rate

3.3 Regulator routing suite (FM/ACBE/MVR)
Goal: ensure triggers fire for correct reasons.

Tests:

FM-Female: missing authority/reservoir

FM-Male: missing ontology/superset

ACBE: capability unknown/below threshold

MVR: no desire → activation path

Metrics:

correct trigger selection rate

false positive/negative rate (include hard negatives)

3.4 RPM quality suite
Goal: ensure RPM outputs actionable, bounded plans.

Checks:

depth and breadth within caps

each prerequisite is testable

no redundant loops

terminal prerequisites map to Action Types (Module 1)

Metrics:

actionability score

redundancy rate

average depth/breadth

3.5 Memory reconstruction fidelity suite
Goal: ensure compressed packets reconstruct correctly at precision p.

Tests:

p=1 gist matches target

p=2 expands key prerequisites

p=3 links raw evidence IDs

lossiness estimates correlate with reconstruction error

Metrics:

reconstruction similarity score

evidence-link coverage

3.6 High-Stakes enforcement suite
Goal: ensure Ask/Slow/Verify + gating is enforced.

Tests:

Normal → output only (NORMAL) when required

High-Stakes → includes ASK→SLOW→VERIFY + PAUSE

Critical → includes -EXECUTE and blocks tools until verified

Metrics:

gate correctness rate

violation rate (must be near 0)

4) Replay harness: how it runs
4.1 Inputs
Past episodes (goals, traces, evidence)

A policy variant:

router rules

verifier thresholds

capability thresholds

reward weights

prompt templates

4.2 Execution
For each episode:

restore initial memory snapshot pointer (or use read-only memory)

run RPM + Router + Executor in sandbox

run Verifier gates

compute reward (Module 4)

log ReplayResult

4.3 Comparison (baseline vs candidate)
Compute deltas:

Δsuccess rate

Δreward mean/median

Δcost (latency/tool calls)

Δuncertainty reduction

Δviolation rate

Accept candidate only if:

violations do not increase

reward improves OR cost drops without reducing success

high-stakes compliance remains perfect (or improves)

5) Promotion gate for Shadow Artifacts
Before promoting a Shadow Artifact → Prime Desire:

attach a replay set of similar episodes + hard negatives

require:

verifier PASS in relevant cases

no increase in violation rate

improvement in expected reward or cost

6) Minimal implementation checklist
Store and label test cases (JSONL or DB)

Implement strict-output validator for format-driven tests

Build replay runner to execute policy variants safely

Compute metrics and generate a report summary

Wire replay pass/fail into:

policy deployment

promotion to Prime Desire

End of Module 5.
"""

This module makes the system debuggable, auditable, and improvable. It logs:

why a decision was made (traceability)

MEMORY_WRITE

  "lossiness": 0.31

3) Traceability requirements (non-negotiable)

the goal/input that caused it

the RPM node(s) that generated it

the verifier result and gate

evidence IDs supporting claims

policy versions used

If any part is missing in High-Stakes/Critical:

automatically downgrade to PAUSE/BLOCK and request missing evidence.

4) Replayability (ties to Module 5)
To replay an episode:

store the full event stream

store tool inputs/outputs (or hashes + cached results)

store memory snapshot pointer (or a read-only view)

store policy version IDs

the same inputs produce the same gates and the same decisions within tolerance.

5) Monitoring and dashboards (what to watch)

success rate by lane (Foundation primary)

average reward and components (Module 4)

high-stakes gate correctness (must be near 100%)

verifier fail rates (soft vs hard)

tool failure rates by tool/domain

average RPM depth/breadth

memory write volume and lossiness distribution

promotions: shadow→prime rate + rollback rate

6) Rollback and “do-not-apply” guards
Whenever a promoted change is deployed:

record a deployment event with version bump

attach acceptance replay set ID

allow instant rollback if:

violation increases

success drops beyond threshold

high-stakes compliance degrades

“Do-not-apply” guard:

store negative patterns with conditions

router must check guard list before applying templates

7) Privacy / safety boundaries
(You can tune this, but it should exist.)

Mark sensitive evidence

Avoid storing unnecessary personal content

Allow redaction in logs (but keep hashes for integrity)

8) Minimal implementation checklist
Event schema + append-only storage

Correlation IDs for every episode and tool call

Policy version stamping

Trace viewer: “input → output path”

Replay export format for Module 5

Rollback + do-not-apply guard list

End of Module 6.
"""
